<!DOCTYPE html>
<html xmlns:th="http://www.thymeleaf.org">
<body>
<div th:fragment="tabla">
  <div class="table-responsive">
    <table class="table table-hover align-middle mb-0">
      <thead class="table-light">
        <tr>
          <th style="width:6%">IMG</th>
          <th style="width:24%">PRODUCTO</th>
          <th style="width:12%">SKU</th>
          <th style="width:12%">MARCA</th>
          <th style="width:10%">VARIANTES</th>
          <th style="width:12%">PRECIO</th>
          <th style="width:10%">ESTADO</th>
          <th style="width:14%" class="text-end">ACCIONES</th>
        </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
        <tr th:if="${#lists.isEmpty(productos)}">
          <td colspan="8" class="text-center text-muted py-4">No se encontraron productos.</td>
        </tr>
        <tr th:each="p : ${productos}">
          <td>
            <img th:if="${p.fotoPrincipal != null}" th:src="${p.fotoPrincipal}"
                 alt="img" style="width:42px;height:42px;object-fit:cover;border:1px solid #dee2e6">
            <span th:unless="${p.fotoPrincipal != null}" class="text-muted">
              <i class="fa-regular fa-image"></i>
            </span>
          </td>
          <td>
            <span class="fw-semibold" th:text="${p.nombre}">Nombre</span>
            <div class="small text-muted text-truncate" style="max-width:240px"
                 th:text="${p.descripcion} ?: ''">desc</div>
          </td>
          <td th:text="${p.sku}">SKU</td>
          <td th:text="${p.marca} ?: '—'">Marca</td>
          <td><span class="badge bg-light text-dark border" th:text="${p.totalVariantes}">0</span></td>
          <td>S/ <span th:text="${#numbers.formatDecimal(p.precioVenta,1,2)}">0.00</span></td>
          <td>
            <span class="badge rounded-pill bg-success" th:if="${p.estado == 'ACTIVO'}">ACTIVO</span>
            <span class="badge rounded-pill bg-warning text-dark" th:if="${p.estado == 'SUSPENDIDO'}">OCULTO</span>
            <span class="badge rounded-pill bg-dark" th:if="${p.estado == 'AGOTADO'}">AGOTADO</span>
            <span class="badge rounded-pill bg-secondary" th:if="${p.estado == 'INACTIVO'}">INACTIVO</span>
          </td>
          <td class="text-end">
            <button type="button" class="btn btn-sm btn-light border"
                    th:attr="hx-get=|/modulo/productos?action=detalle&id=${p.idProducto}|"
                    hx-target="#zp-modal-slot" hx-swap="innerHTML" title="Ver">
              <i class="fa-solid fa-eye text-info"></i>
            </button>
            <button type="button" class="btn btn-sm btn-primary"
                    th:attr="hx-get=|/modulo/productos?action=form&id=${p.idProducto}|"
                    hx-target="#zp-modal-slot" hx-swap="innerHTML" title="Editar">
              <i class="fa-solid fa-pen"></i>
            </button>
            <button type="button" class="btn btn-sm btn-danger"
                    th:if="${p.estado == 'ACTIVO'}"
                    th:attr="hx-post=|/modulo/productos|,
                             hx-vals=|{&quot;action&quot;:&quot;toggle&quot;,&quot;idProducto&quot;:${p.idProducto}}|"
                    hx-target="#zp-productos-tabla" hx-swap="innerHTML"
                    hx-confirm="¿Ocultar este producto?" title="Ocultar">
              <i class="fa-solid fa-trash"></i>
            </button>
            <button type="button" class="btn btn-sm btn-success"
                    th:unless="${p.estado == 'ACTIVO'}"
                    th:attr="hx-post=|/modulo/productos|,
                             hx-vals=|{&quot;action&quot;:&quot;toggle&quot;,&quot;idProducto&quot;:${p.idProducto}}|"
                    hx-target="#zp-productos-tabla" hx-swap="innerHTML"
                    hx-confirm="¿Reactivar este producto?" title="Reactivar">
              <i class="fa-solid fa-rotate-left"></i>
            </button>
          </td>
        </tr>
      </tbody>
    </table>
  </div>

      Página <span th:text="${pageActual + 1}">1</span> de
      <span th:text="${totalPaginas == 0 ? 1 : totalPaginas}">1</span>
      (<span th:text="${totalRegistros}">0</span> productos)
    </span>
    <div class="btn-group">
      <button type="button" class="btn btn-sm btn-light border"
              th:disabled="${pageActual == 0}"
              th:attr="hx-get=|/modulo/productos?action=table&q=${filtro}&estado=${fEstado}&size=${size}&sort=${sort}&dir=${dir}&page=${pageActual - 1}|"
              hx-target="#zp-productos-tabla" hx-swap="innerHTML">
        <i class="fa-solid fa-chevron-left"></i>
      </button>
      <button type="button" class="btn btn-sm btn-light border"
              th:disabled="${pageActual + 1 >= totalPaginas}"
              th:attr="hx-get=|/modulo/productos?action=table&q=${filtro}&estado=${fEstado}&size=${size}&sort=${sort}&dir=${dir}&page=${pageActual + 1}|"
              hx-target="#zp-productos-tabla" hx-swap="innerHTML">
        <i class="fa-solid fa-chevron-right"></i>
      </button>
    </div>

<div xmlns:th="http://www.thymeleaf.org"
     xmlns:sec="http://www.thymeleaf.org/extras/spring-security"
     th:fragment="tabla" id="zp-usuarios-tabla" class="table-responsive">
    <table class="table table-hover align-middle">
        <thead class="table-light">
            <tr>
                <th>USUARIO</th>
                <th>NOMBRE COMPLETO</th>
                <th>DNI</th>
                <th>PERFIL</th>
                <th>ESTADO</th>
                <th>FECHA DE CREACIÓN</th>
                <th class="text-end">ACCIONES</th>
            </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
            <tr th:if="${#lists.isEmpty(usuarios)}">
                <td colspan="7" class="text-center text-muted py-4">Sin resultados</td>
            </tr>
            <tr th:each="u : ${usuarios}">
                <td>
                    <div class="fw-semibold" th:text="${u.username}">user</div>
                    <div class="small text-muted" th:text="${u.email}">email</div>
                </td>
                <td th:text="${u.nombreCompleto}">Nombre</td>
                <td th:text="${u.dni}">00000000</td>
                <td th:text="${u.rolNombre}">Rol</td>
                <td>
                    <span th:if="${u.estado == 'ACTIVO'}" class="badge rounded-pill text-bg-success">ACTIVO</span>
                    <span th:if="${u.estado != 'ACTIVO'}" class="badge rounded-pill text-bg-secondary">INACTIVO</span>
                </td>
                <td th:text="${#temporals.format(u.createdAt, 'dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm')}">fecha</td>
                <td class="text-end">
                    <button class="btn btn-sm btn-primary"
                            sec:authorize="hasAuthority('USUARIOS_EDITAR') or hasRole('ADMIN')"
                            th:hx-get="@{/modulo/usuarios/form(id=${u.idUsuario})}"
                            hx-target="#zp-modal-slot" hx-swap="innerHTML" title="Editar">
                        <i class="fa-solid fa-pen"></i>
                    </button>
                    
                    <button class="btn btn-sm btn-danger"
                            sec:authorize="hasAuthority('USUARIOS_ELIMINAR') or hasRole('ADMIN')"
                            th:if="${u.estado == 'ACTIVO' and u.rolCodigo != 'ADMIN'}"
                            th:hx-post="@{/modulo/usuarios/delete}"
                            th:hx-vals="|{&quot;id&quot;: ${u.idUsuario}}|"
                            hx-target="#zp-usuarios-tabla" hx-swap="outerHTML"
                            hx-confirm="¿Desactivar este usuario?" title="Desactivar">
                        <i class="fa-solid fa-trash"></i>
                    </button>
                </td>
            </tr>
        </tbody>
    </table>
</div>
